# eof: the end-of-frame lead group. Follow-the-wire pieces that no other package draws
# (queue row `eof` of notes/work-packages.md). Notes: notes/blocks/eof.md.
# All coordinates are metal-shot pixels. Images named here are in cache/scratch/eof/.
@block eof
# rdp3 draws the stretch between this file's two links (L7 at y 10700 to L9 at y 13480); it is
# loaded so that the package extracts as one net when read by itself (TRACE=eof).
@requires rdp3
# lc2 holds the cell lc2_ff, placed again in piece ff3 (and lc1 the rails and names lc2 hangs from)
@requires lc2
@requires lc1
# bd1 holds the latch on R4's match, whose lower half's load is piece r4lat
@requires bd1
@w M=18 P=18
@s 26x26

# Piece r4: R4's match (rc_eq_r4) from the counter channel to bd1's latch. Wiring only.
#
# The line's path, north to south (bundle tracks L0..L11 at x 13348 + 43.4 k):
#   rdp2 makes it; mrach brings it down track T6 (= L0) to the contact (13349,9663) and draws

#   bd1: k15, jog west at y 15087, contact (13655,15391), the poly of gate P2 (the latch's
#     upper half, in series with rst_lat) and of P4 (inverter A).

# In the full extraction rst_ck (then under three names; the clock block's and inrow's name for the
# net of trunks' line S) are one net. Read by itself this file holds two pieces of it.
